| D-M4-18-12 | 2026-10-17 | The new pub packages of `apps/client` and their transitive packages get one `PackageOverrides` entry each in the root `osv-scanner.toml` (name and exact version), checked against the LICENSE file of that version: MIT (Riverpod, `state_notifier`, `uuid`, `freezed_annotation`, `analyzer_buffer`), Apache-2.0 (`mockito`) and BSD-3-Clause (the flutter.dev and Dart team packages, `flutter_secure_storage`, `ffi_leak_tracker`, `win32`). `quality/licenses/allowed.txt` is unchanged. | deps.dev has no license data for these pub packages, so OSV-Scanner reports them as UNKNOWN (docs/quality/licenses.md). |
| D-M4-18-13 | 2026-10-17 | The app remembers the public description of the last instance discovery found (`app/instance`: address, `instance_id`, product name, accent, login methods; no logo, no secret). A start or "Connect" whose discovery gets no answer at all (`DiscoveryUnreachable`: no network, host down) opens the remembered instance from its local data when its address matches and a refresh token is stored, and shows the main view (or E4 for a waiting device); otherwise E1 shows "No instance at this address". A found instance with a stored session resumes on every connect, not only at the start. | 01 3.6 (offline reading) and 6.6 (the offline banner): a signed-in person must reach the local data without network. |
| D-M4-18-14 | 2026-10-17 | Fixes after the red team: E1 leaves the "connecting" state when discovery found the instance and on E2 "Change"; stored column widths are fitted to the window (`fitColumns`: the sized columns shrink in proportion to their width above `DsLayout.columnMin`, so the history keeps its minimum); the onboarding card runs its main action on Enter only from the card itself or a text field, a focused button or radio keeps Enter for itself, and the text fields no longer submit on their own, so one Enter is one action. | Red team round 1 (four reproduced Majors), each with a test. |
| D-M4-18-15 | 2026-10-17 | One pure function `routeStart` (`apps/client/lib/onboarding/start_route.dart`) decides the screen of every start and "Connect" from what is stored for the instance (no session, a session of an active device, a session of a waiting device), the discovery result and what `getDevice` says about the device. A certificate error, `setup_required` and a too old app show E1 with their text; a stored session of the remembered instance at that address otherwise opens its local data whatever else the network answered (no answer, a timeout, a `4xx` or `5xx` page of a reverse proxy, a captive portal): the main view, or E4 for a waiting device, where the server's answer about the device wins over the stored state; without a session a found instance shows E2 and everything else "No instance at this address". E4 stores its verification code and `created_at` as the instance secret `onboarding/pending_device` (deleted at the login and by the wiper), so a waiting device started offline shows E4 with its code and polls until the network returns. This refines D-M4-18-13, which opened the local data only without any answer. A table test runs every stored state times every network result times "comes online" or "stays offline" through the controller against the fake server. | Delta red team: a proxy's `502`, `503`, `504` sent a signed-in person to E1, and a waiting device started offline landed in the empty main view; both were instances of one routing rule spread over two places. |
| D-M4-18-16 | 2026-10-18 | The ticket was ported from its integration base onto main with only its own commits, stacked on the port of M4-17; the English docs of main win: the two paragraphs of 15 9 and 15 11 sit in the English chapter, and code comments point to `01-product.md` and `15-client-architecture.md`. The lock file is resolved again on main (`flutter pub get`, only `dart_style` differs from the base, as on main), the two goldens of E1 with the "app too old" banner are rerendered, and the goldens compare with `DsGoldenFileComparator` (PL/5). `sonar.coverage.exclusions` leaves `apps/*/macos/**`, `apps/*/windows/**` and `apps/*/tool/**` out of the coverage measure. The skip line of the Riverpod generation step is `codegen-riverpod-dart`, like the drift and Pigeon steps. `osv-scanner.toml` gets the license overrides of `flutter_driver`, `integration_test`, `fuchsia_remote_debug_protocol`, `flutter_web_plugins`, `sync_http` and `webdriver`, which the base had from M6-12 and M6-02. The fake server of M4-17 lists the local login driver in `auth_providers` of discovery, as the server does (`loginoptions` lists every enabled provider); without it E2 showed no login name and every integration test of the app failed on the desktop runners. | Main carries its own value of `color.warning.subtle` (`palette.amber.100`), which the banner shows (as D-M4-07-9 found); without the comparator the CI goldens recorded on macOS fail on the Linux runner at anti-aliased edges, as those of design_system and bothy_viz did. The runners run only on the platform runners and write no report the Linux scan reads (as D-M4-10-7 for the plugin members), and `flutter test --coverage` records only `lib/`, so the tested branding tool counted as uncovered (as D-M4-06-8 for the members' tools). |
| D-M4-18-17 | 2026-10-19 | The fake server of `sync_engine` carries a gzip and base64 copy of `docs/api/openapi.yaml` and `docs/api/asyncapi.yaml` in `lib/src/testing/contract_documents.g.dart` (`ContractSchemas.embedded`); `ContractSchemas.load` still walks up to `docs/api/` and uses the copy only when there is none. `tools/catalog/contract_documents_dart.py` writes it (`make contract-documents`), and `make codegen-check` decodes it and fails when its text differs from `docs/api/`, so a change to the API documents needs a regeneration. The macOS app sandbox stays on in every build configuration; no asset is added to the app. | Steering ruling 2026-10-19: the app's integration tests ran on a device for the first time, and inside the macOS app sandbox the fake could not read the contract files (working directory `/`, no access to the repository), so no route and no contract check existed. A Flutter asset would ship 1 MB of YAML in every release build, a sandbox exception would weaken debug builds; the copy is test code in `package:sync_engine/testing.dart`, which no release build imports (tools/arch-check rule 10). The copy is random-looking text, so `.gitleaks.toml` allows its path; `docs/api/` itself stays scanned. |
| D-M4-18-18 | 2026-10-19 | The Windows failure "file in use" when a test deleted its data folder is a test teardown issue, not a product one: the product wipe (`AccountWiper`, every sign-out cause) awaits the engine stop and `LocalDatabase.wipe`, which closes the database before it deletes the files, and only then deletes the instance folder; `logout_wipes_test.dart` checks on the Windows job that the folder is gone. Only a test that removes the app leaves the close to the controller's dispose, which does not wait (the app quits). `FakeInstance.stop` retries the delete, within a bounded time, until that close is done. | Steering ruling 2026-10-19: check the product path first and fix it if it has the same bug; reading the code and the device run of the wipe test show it does not. |
| D-M4-18-19 | 2026-10-19 | `apps/client/platform-tests.json` runs each integration test file in its own `flutter test <file> -d macos` or `-d windows` call; a new file needs its line there. | On both desktop runners every file after the first failed with "Unable to start the app on the device" (the log reader of the next app start stopped) so each file gets its own call and a fresh app process, and a failure names its file. |
| D-M4-18-20 | 2026-10-19 | Discovery accepts an `instance_id` only as a UUID (`canonicalUuid` of `domain`, stored in lowercase); any other value is "no instance" in E1, and a remembered instance whose ID is no UUID is not read back. | Review finding on PR #138: the ID names the data folder, the device key scope and the secret prefix, and the wipe deletes that folder recursively, so a server answering `..` could make a sign-out delete data outside its instance. |

## M4-25 Desktop settings, own devices and sessions, step-up dialog

| No | Date | Ruling | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-M4-25-0 | 2026-10-17 | Base `int/m1-wave38` at `297ab0c`, branch `feat/M4-25-settings-devices` in its own worktree; the branch is not merged locally and not pushed, the PR lane lands it. The `/settings/<section>` routes are registered in `app.dart` next to the other `GoRoute`s, because `router.dart` only parses locations (D-M4-18-4). | The task of the build lane overrides the merge step of the brief; the router file has no route table. Sources: brief M4-25 Boundaries. |
| D-M4-25-1 | 2026-10-17 | Settings defaults live on the client (`PersonalSettings.resolve`): an absent key or a value outside the list of 01 11 reads as the default; `composer.enter_sends` defaults to `true` on desktop, `privacy.crash_reports` to `true` only with a crash target, `privacy.client_metrics` to `true`. Choosing a device-independent default deletes the key (`deleteMySetting`), every other change is one `putMySetting` through the outbox; the local row changes at once. "Column widths: Reset" deletes `layout.column_widths`. | 04 14 stores no defaults, because some depend on the device or the instance. Sources: 01 11, 04 14, brief M4-25 Design note 1. |
| D-M4-25-2 | 2026-10-17 | The device and session screens live in `packages/admin_ui/lib/src/self/` and read and write only through `SelfRepository` (REST over `api_client`, no local database); the desktop hosts them in Settings, the console (M6) and the mobile emergency actions (M5) reuse them. | 15 3.1: `admin_ui` depends on `api_client`, `auth`, `design_system`, `l10n`, never on `sync_engine` or `local_store` (`make arch`). Sources: 15 3, 3.1. |
| D-M4-25-3 | 2026-10-17 | `StepUpDialog` and `StepUpDialogPrompt` (the `StepUpPrompt` of M4-09) live in `admin_ui`; `AppPorts` installs the prompt over the router's navigator key, replacing the `ConfirmingStepUpPrompt` of M4-18. The dialog appears only when the server answers `401 step_up_required` (`device.approve`, `device.revoke`); ending a session asks one confirmation and never for step-up. On a pairing `404` the server ends the step-up of the session (the fake server does the same), so the next attempt asks again (08 5.3). | Step-up handling stays in the `StepUpService` of M4-09; the screen adds no step-up of its own (05). Sources: 08 5.2, 5.3, 6; brief M4-25 Design note 3. |
| D-M4-25-4 | 2026-10-17 | `privacy.crash_reports` is shown only when the build names a crash target: `BuildBranding.hasCrashTarget` from the define `BRANDING_CRASH_REPORTS=true` until M4-31 brings the target itself; absent means hidden. `privacy.client_metrics` is always shown. | 01 11 and 15 16: crash reports only with a configured target. Sources: brief M4-25 Design note 4. |
| D-M4-25-5 | 2026-10-17 | The device list shows no hardware binding label: the `Device` schema of `docs/api/` has no hardware or attestation field. Revoking this device or ending the current session calls `OnboardingController.endLocally`, which runs the wiper of M4-18 without a server logout and shows E7. Time zones come from the IANA database of the `timezone` package. | 01 A1 names the label, but the contract does not carry it; the gap is reported, not built (P-11). Sources: 01 5.1 D8, 08 10, R-SY-14. |
| D-M4-25-6 | 2026-10-19 | Port onto main from the integration squash of `int/m1-wave40` on top of M4-18: the ticket's own commits only, with the doc comment fix of INT-W40/4 (`ApiSelfRepository` names its constructor argument as `` `api` ``); the rulings stand in their own section; the move of the early rulings to `DECISIONS-EARLY.md` (INT-W40/3) is not ported, because main's log is below `repo.max_file_kb`. | The PR lane ports only the ticket and the integration fixes it needs. |
| D-M4-25-7 | 2026-10-19 | The integration tests `devices_test.dart` and `settings_sync_test.dart` of this ticket get their lines in `apps/client/platform-tests.json`, so the platform jobs run them on macOS and Windows; the source references of D-M4-25-0 to D-M4-25-5 move into the reason cell, as the table has four columns. | D-M4-18-19 asks a line for each new file; the register check refuses a row with more cells than its header. |
| D-M4-25-8 | 2026-10-19 | Ending the own session from the settings ends in E7 at once: `GatewayConnection.dispose` no longer waits for the close of its state stream, and `OnboardingController` sets no state after its provider was disposed; the platform test of the theme reset taps the `system` choice inside `settings.theme`. | The first platform runs of `devices_test.dart` hung 20 seconds in the wipe: the status line provider listens to the state stream and Riverpod pauses it while the settings cover the shell, and the close of a broadcast stream waits for paused listeners; the late `state` write then threw `UnmountedRefException`. Theme and motion both offer `system`, so the old finder matched two chips. Tests: `session_test.dart` (a paused listener does not hold dispose) and `controller_test.dart` (an end that outlives its container). |
| D-M4-25-9 | 2026-10-20 | `FakeInstance.stopSibling` deletes the data folder of the second app with the retry of `stop` (D-M4-18-18), until its database is closed or `closeTimeout` ends. | On the Windows runner `settings_sync_test.dart` failed in its teardown with `PathAccessException` (errno 32): the disposed container closes the database without waiting, and Windows refuses to delete an open file. Both deletes now share one helper. |
| D-M4-25-10 | 2026-10-20 | `L10nFormat.time` puts the no-break space U+00A0 before AM and PM instead of the U+202F that `intl` writes; the goldens with English clock times are recorded again (settings devices, `admin_ui` self devices and sessions, the macOS goldens of six `bothy_viz` views). `admin_ui` installs `DsGoldenFileComparator` in its `flutter_test_config.dart` like the other members with goldens (PL/5). | Atkinson Hyperlegible Next has no U+202F, so every host drew it with its own fallback font: on the Linux runner the device rows of `settings_devices` grew wider and wrapped, and the CI golden failed. The new goldens of `admin_ui` had never run on Linux and failed there without the comparator of PL/5. Both were reproduced in a Linux amd64 container with Flutter 3.47.7, where all goldens of `apps/client`, `admin_ui` and `bothy_viz` pass after the change. Test: `formatting_test.dart`. |

## M7-11 API breaking change check

| No | Date | Ruling | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-M7-11-0 | 2026-10-16 | The ticket was built on an integration base and ported onto main with only its own commits; the English docs and spec of main win, and text this ticket adds is English. The rows stand in a section per ticket, as for the other ported tickets. No contract change of `main` is needed: the brief relies on 06 13 and 15. | The tickets are built in parallel on integration bases; main takes each ticket as one pull request; merged names win (D-W-100). |
| D-M7-11-1 | 2026-10-15 | Tool (Design note 1): G6 is the own comparison `tools/api-checks/breaking.py` over the parsed YAML (PyYAML, already locked), not oasdiff; nothing new is pinned. Evidence: oasdiff 1.33.0 (`darwin_all` release asset) parsed `docs/api/openapi.yaml` and reported "No changes detected" against itself, but on mutated copies it reported no breaking change for a removed property of `TypingStartedPayload` (an event schema no path references) and for a changed `x-authz-action` of `GET /instance/discovery`, and it reported a removed query parameter `cursor` only as a warning (`request-parameter-removed`); it did report a removed `201` as an error. A wrapper would have needed its own comparison for the events, the action and the pagination kind anyway. | 06 13 item 7 leaves the tool open; Design note 1 asks for the own comparison when oasdiff misses a row; the rows are structural and few. |
| D-M7-11-2 | 2026-10-15 | Base (requirement 12): the newest stable tag `server/vX.Y.Z` (SemVer precedence) among the tags merged into `HEAD`, read with `git show <tag>:docs/api/openapi.yaml` (and `asyncapi.yaml` for directions when present); any pre-release tag, not only `-beta.N`, is the base only when no stable tag is merged, the newest by SemVer precedence; no tag gives the notice `no published version` and exit 0; a tag without `docs/api/openapi.yaml` is an error. Only merged tags count, like `last_stable_tag` of `tools/ci/release_prep.py`, so a maintenance branch compares with its own line; CI checks out with `fetch-depth: 0`, so the tags are there. The gate runs when `server` is selected; a change under `docs/api/` selects every gate component. | Brief requirement 12 and Design note 3; the API document ships with the server (`RELEASE_EXTRA_PATHS`). |
| D-M7-11-3 | 2026-10-15 | Major version exception (requirement 14): the gate accepts the findings, printed as `accepted`, only when the major of `info.version` of the working tree is higher than that of the base, also from `0.x` to `1.0.0`; a minor step under `0.x` does not accept a break, and an `info.version` that is not SemVer is an error. Rules of the comparison that the 06 table leaves open: a schema's direction comes from paths, `webhooks` (reversed) and `asyncapi.yaml`, and a schema without one counts in both directions; a response property that is no longer required counts as row 1, a changed `default` as row 3 and, for a parameter named `sort`, also as row 6; a changed `operationId` is a rename (row 1); the pagination kind comes from the query parameters `cursor`, `before_seq`, `after_seq`, `around_seq` and an integer `after` (a time filter `after` is none); the schema of a response header is compared like a field; a removed response status is row 5, a new status passes; a changed status of a problem code or `x-status` of a response component is row 5, a removed problem code row 1. `asyncapi.yaml` changes of channels, operations and messages are not covered (brief Out of scope). | 06 13 items 4 and 5; requirement 14 says nothing else lowers the gate; an unknown direction must not hide a new required request field. |

## M4-05 Icons in the design system

| No | Date | Ruling | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-M4-05-0 | 2026-10-17 | The ticket was built on an integration base and ported onto main with only its own commits; the English docs and spec of main win, and text this ticket adds is English. The lock keeps `vector_graphics_compiler` 1.3.1, the version the ticket measured and generated the assets with; `http`, which `vector_graphics` brings, already has its license override from M4-06, whose reason now names both users. | The tickets are built in parallel on integration bases; main takes each ticket as one pull request; merged names win (D-W-100). |
| D-M4-05-1 | 2026-10-15 | The icons ship as `vector_graphics_compiler` assets (`assets/icons/<weight>/<glyph>.vec`), not as an icon font. Measured on 128 glyphs at 16, 20 and 24 px and ratios 1 and 2 against the `flutter_svg` render: vectors 0 % mean alpha difference everywhere, the font 2.97 % mean and up to 47.1 % in one pixel at 16 px x1 (table in docs/design/icons.md "Integration (M4-05)"); 74,608 bytes for the vectors against 34,564 for the font. | Design note 1: the clearly sharper candidate at 16 px wins, the size only decides a tie. `vector_graphics` is maintained by the Flutter team (BSD-3-Clause). |
| D-M4-05-2 | 2026-10-15 | The icon assets, their lookup file, LICENSE and MANIFEST.json are generated by the token tool (`tokens.py icons`, part of `build`); `check` rebuilds them in memory and compares byte for byte, an extra file under `assets/icons/` is stale too. The compiler runs from the workspace lock with the mask, clip and overdraw optimizers off. | docs/design/icons.md names `icon.tokens.json` as the single source; the optimizers need the native path-ops library of the host, and Phosphor glyphs have no masks or clips, so turning them off keeps the bytes the same on every host. |
| D-M4-05-3 | 2026-10-15 | The assets hold every mapped glyph in its mapped weight and in `fill`, and every space icon in `regular`; `tokens.py vendor` copies exactly these SVGs and no longer requires `--sans` and `--mono` (without them `vendor/fonts/` stays as it is). | Design note 3: the golden and `DsIcon(filled: true)` need the active form of every icon; a new icon should need only the Phosphor package. |
| D-M4-05-4 | 2026-10-15 | A spinning glyph rotates once per `motion.duration.pulse`, and only while the ambient `DsMotion` has a positive pulse, which only the full context has (the reduced one is zero); otherwise no `AnimationController` exists. | No dedicated token exists, and adding one would change the token files of M0-01's gate (design note 4). The pulse test also holds during a theme animation, when `AnimatedTheme` lerps a new `DsMotion` instance that is not identical to `DsMotion.full`. |
| D-M4-05-5 | 2026-10-15 | `DsIcon` throws an `ArgumentError` without a non-empty `label` and without `decorative: true` in debug and in release (no separate assert); an empty label counts as none. `DsIconSizeRole` is a new enum of this ticket (the brief names it, the generated code has only `DsIconSize`). `testing.dart` gains `dsPumpIcons`, so `flutter_test` (SDK) becomes a regular dependency like `alchemist`, and `DsIconPlaceholder` is public so the helper can wait for it. The lookup class is `DsIconAssets` with `lookup(glyph, {filled})` and `space(name)`, returning the asset path. | One rule in every build mode; an empty label gives no screen reader text. Vector assets load asynchronously, and every member with icons in a golden needs the same wait. |
| D-M4-05-6 | 2026-10-17 | The pre-push hook runs `make check` as `env -u GIT_DIR -u GIT_WORK_TREE -u GIT_INDEX_FILE -u GIT_COMMON_DIR -u GIT_PREFIX make check`; `tools/hooks/tests/test_pre_push_env.py` runs the hook's prefix with the five variables set and holds that none reaches the command. The per-tool removals (D-M2-38-22, `codegen_check.py`, `dart_api_docs.py`) stay. | Fourth tool with the same failure: `tokens.py` runs `dart run vector_graphics_compiler`, and under the hook the Dart SDK read its version from this repository's GIT_DIR and refused the workspace ("requires the Flutter SDK"), so the token gate failed in every pre-push run while it passed in CI. One rule at the hook covers every gate instead of each tool. |

## M4-07 Core components with goldens

| No | Date | Ruling | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-M4-07-0 | 2026-10-15 | Base `int/m1-wave24` at `2a72cca`, branch `feat/M4-07-core-components` in its own worktree; the branch is not merged locally and not pushed, the PR lane ports it. Text this ticket adds is English, also inside the German chapter `docs/design/components.md`. Names that differ on this base and are followed: the chapters are `01-produkt.md` and `15-client-architektur.md`; the trigger state widget is `DsTriggerStateIcon`, because `DsTriggerState` is the enum of the view models. No core rule or ruling from main was needed (R-PR-04, D-W-100 and D-W-114 are on the base). | Instructions of the launcher, which override the brief's landing step; merged names win (D-W-100, D-W-114). |
| D-M4-07-1 | 2026-10-15 | Components take small immutable view models defined in `design_system` (`view_models.dart`: `DsMessageData`, `DsRunData`, `DsStepData`, `DsThreadRowData`, `DsChannelRowData`, `DsSpaceRowData`, `DsComponentData`, `DsComponentHeader`, `DsSuggestion`, `DsComposerCommand` and the enums `DsMessageOrigin`, `DsMessageKind`, `DsTriggerState`, `DsRunState`, `DsStepKind`, `DsStepStatus`, `DsBillingMode`, `DsComponentType`, `DsComponentState`, `DsButtonStyle`, `DsApprover`, `DsChannelKind`, `DsFieldType`, `DsCategory`). Enum values carry the codes of 03 and an `unknown` member (`DsCategory` falls back to `granite`); `fromCode` never throws. Callbacks are plain functions; no component performs I/O or imports a provider. | Brief note 1: testable with literal data, usable by the console and the golden harness; a newer server may send a new code (06 13). |
| D-M4-07-2 | 2026-10-15 | `DsMarkdown` parses with package `markdown` (GitHub flavored without the inline HTML syntaxes) and builds its own widgets for the subset of the brief. HTML renders as literal text; an image renders as the text "Image: <alt> (<domain>)" and never loads; links are active only for `https`, `http`, `mailto` and the internal paths `/t/` and `/c/`, show their domain (or the internal target) on hover and focus and reach `onOpenLink` only on activation; every other scheme renders as plain text; headings are at most `type.heading`; code blocks scroll horizontally and copy exactly the code. `markdown` 7.3.1 gets a checked `PackageOverrides` entry (BSD-3-Clause, from its LICENSE file) in `osv-scanner.toml`, because deps.dev knows no license for it, as D-M4-04-5 does for alchemist. Block quotes and lists nest as structure up to `DsMarkdown.maxNesting` (8) levels; a deeper element renders as its plain text, because the server accepts any nesting and thousands of levels froze the interface (red team of M4-07). | Brief note 2: a widget package would decide link and image handling and could load resources (SEC-OUT-1, SEC-OUT-2). |
| D-M4-07-3 | 2026-10-15 | `DsMessageComponents` draws a framed header with `security.step-up` built only from `DsComponentHeader` (agent, run, required capability, approval note), then a divider labeled "Text from <agent>", then the agent text as Markdown, then the components. `DsMessage` therefore does not draw the content of a `kind = card` message that has components. After the first activation the widget ignores every further one until the caller reports a new state of the card (another `state`, `pendingCustomId`, `errorText` or message); an equal view model rebuilt by the parent keeps the lock, and `pendingCustomId` from the caller locks the same way. For screen readers the group is labeled by the server header and the header is one node with the plain names; the agent's card title is read after the divider, and the message label of a card names only author and time. `onActivate` gets null for a button, the list of chosen values for a select and the field map for a form. Only Enter or Space on the focused button activates (the default `ActivateIntent`); no other key or shortcut does. | Brief note 5, SEC-OUT-4 and SEC-T14 at component level; 01 6.5 (a double click creates nothing twice); R-PR-04. |
| D-M4-07-4 | 2026-10-15 | `LiveAnnouncer` ignores the first observation, announces a change of the state text or a new question ID, never a patch of steps or numbers, and merges announcements closer than `minInterval` (`a11y.live_announce_min_interval`) into the latest candidate, which `flush` sends once the interval has passed; a state candidate equal to the last sent text is dropped, a new question never is (all questions of an agent read the same). The live box arms the flush timer again when it fires early. The live box sends through `SemanticsService.announce` with polite assertiveness, in all three forms: the collapsed and the mobile line also take `announceMinInterval`, so the end of a run and questions on a phone are announced. | Brief note 4, 01 8 and components.md 2.4. |
| D-M4-07-5 | 2026-10-15 | The golden matrix is one sheet per component (live box and components on messages split into an active and a final sheet), and one file per sheet, density and theme, each holding text scales 1 and 2 and the locales `de` and `en`: 9 sheets, 54 files for CI and 54 for macOS, the largest file below 700 KB, together about 22 MB (CI about 2.5 MB). `dsGoldenMatrix` passes the `l10n` delegates with the locale, because the alchemist app has none. `guidelines_test.dart` runs the four Flutter guidelines over the same sheets in light and dark with the `touch` density. | Brief note 8 allows a file per component, theme, density, text scale and locale; one file per component and density reached 2.5 MB and exceeds `repo.max_file_kb`, and no dimension may be dropped. |
| D-M4-07-7 | 2026-10-15 | The message label stays "<Name>, <Time>, <Text>" with the text joined by spaces between blocks, list items and table cells, and the content keeps its own semantics below it, so links (own nodes with a tap action) and the copy button stay reachable; a screen reader may read the text twice. A continuation message shows the trigger state at the end of its row. Trigger and run reasons cover the acceptance reasons of 09 3.5 and `user_cancelled` without a name. | Code review of M4-07: links and the copy button were unreachable; 01 6.3 wants the state at every triggering message. |
| D-M4-07-8 | 2026-10-15 | The composer leaves Enter, Tab, the arrows and Escape to the input method while a composition is active. The generated token files stay byte for byte as `tokens.py build` writes them; `dart format` runs only on hand-written folders. | Red team of M4-07: Enter during an IME composition sent half-typed text; a formatted copy made `make tokens` red. |
| D-M4-07-6 | 2026-10-15 | Flutter 3.47.7 has no tree role in `SemanticsRole`, so space rows are expandable nodes with the expanded or collapsed state and channel rows carry their label; the arrow is `action.expand` (caret down) when expanded and `action.collapse` (caret right) when collapsed, as `icon.tokens.json` maps them. Two opacity values without a token are named constants with a reason: the icon tile of a space (0.16) and the lowest point of the run dot pulse (0.4). | components.md 7 asks for a tree; the semantics that Flutter offers are the expanded state. The token set has no tint or pulse opacity role. |
| D-M4-07-9 | 2026-10-17 | The ticket was ported from its integration base onto main with only its own commits (the integration fixes of the wave concern other tickets); the English docs of main win, so the new paragraphs in `docs/design/components.md` sit under the English headings of main. The nine light goldens of badges, composer and active live box are rerendered on main, macOS and CI variant. | Main carries its own value of `color.warning.subtle` (`palette.amber.100`), light `#FEE6D4` instead of `#FEF3EA` on the integration base; the warning chips of these sheets show it, nothing else changed in the images. |
| D-M4-07-10 | 2026-10-17 | The component sheets are `sheetWidth` wide, wider than the longest scenario label of alchemist, and every golden is rerecorded; the text contrast guideline of `guidelines_test.dart` runs on macOS only, the tap target and label guidelines on every host. CI goldens compare with `DsGoldenFileComparator` (PL/5). | On the Linux runner the light comfortable sheets took the width of their label, a fraction of a pixel wider than a whole number, and the last pixel column stayed transparent there while macOS filled it. The contrast guideline measures the most frequent colors of the rendered glyphs: on Linux a blended edge shade of the link outnumbered `color.text.link` and measured 4.18 although the pair has 5.9 and passes the token gate, which checks every contrast pair on every host. |
| D-M4-07-11 | 2026-10-18 | `DsComposer` clears its own text controller after `onSend`; a controller passed by the caller keeps its text, and the caller clears it. `onChanged` also reports the changes the composer makes itself (the new line of Shift+Enter, the clearing after a send), so a draft or typing state of the caller stays current. | CodeRabbit on the pull request, reproduced with a test: with the documented call without a controller the sent text stayed in the field, and the next Enter sent it again (a duplicate message). |
